Title: La vie de dom Armand-Jean Le Bouthillier de Rancé, abbé régulier et réformateur du monastère de la Trappe, de l'étroite observance de Cîteaux; nouvelle édition; tome premier
Creator: Jacques Marsollier (title page: 'Par M. l'Abbé de Marsollier, Chanoine de l'Eglise Cathédrale d'Usez')
Publisher: Paris: chez Savoye, rue Saint Jacques, à l'Espérance (title page)
Publication Date: 1758 (title page: 'M. DCC. LVIII.', read on the page image, leaf n6; the OCR reads 'M. Dec LVIIL' at line 29)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1758); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: fra
Source: archive.org item laviededomarmand01mars, full text from the item's own djvu.txt (laviededomarmand01mars_djvu.txt). Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (688,718) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1758, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: clean French with the long s read as f, double-spaced words and running heads in the text. Word count of the host text: 105,028. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Books I-III. Title page at OCR lines 4-31. Epistle to the king at 48-330; the author's avertissement at 341-795; table of chapters from 800. Book I: chapter I at 1175, to 'Fin du premier Livre' at 6605 (twenty-one chapters). Book II at 6612-12204 (fifteen chapters). Book III at 12211-17361 (fifteen chapters). 'Fin du Tome premier' at 17362. Letters and documents are quoted inside the chapters.
